# VistA FileMan file #2 (PATIENT) → FHIR R4 Patient: field-by-field mapping

Generated by `build_data.py` from [`Packages/VA FileMan/Globals/DD.zwr`](https://github.com/COG-GTM/VA-VistA-Mumps/blob/b7aecb9029f9bb8639a7bfa63b635469065ab44d/Packages/VA%20FileMan/Globals/DD.zwr) at commit `b7aecb9029`. Do not edit by hand.

524 top-level fields in file #2, plus 14 fields in the ALIAS, RACE, ETHNICITY, LANGUAGE and ICN-history subfiles that feed Patient elements.

| Status | Fields |
|---|---|
| Patient | 173 |
| Partial | 23 |
| Other resource | 99 |
| No FHIR equivalent | 234 |
| Derived | 9 |

**Columns.** *DD line* is the line in `DD.zwr` holding the field's FileMan 0-node (`^DD(file,field,0)` = name^type^set/pointer^node;piece^input transform M code); the text shown is that line verbatim (truncated). *Logic* links each `TAG^ROUTINE` called by the field's input transform to the line defining that tag. `DD.zwr` is too large for GitHub to render, so its line numbers are given as text; check one with `sed -n '<line>p' "Packages/VA FileMan/Globals/DD.zwr"`. Full, untruncated rows are in [`mapping.csv`](mapping.csv).

Patient.id and the MR identifier come from the record IEN (DFN), which is not a field.
